### [1] [NCCLbpf: Verified, Composable Policy Execution for GPU Collective Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11438)
*Yusheng Zheng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: NCCLbpf是一个将eBPF运行时嵌入NCCL插件接口的验证性高性能扩展框架，无需修改NCCL本身即可实现安全、可热更新的插件策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NCCL插件以未验证的原生代码形式在NCCL地址空间内运行，存在作业崩溃、状态静默损坏及策略更新时重启导致停机的风险。

Method: 设计NCCLbpf框架，包含三个核心机制：1) 加载时静态验证防止不安全插件执行；2) 结构化跨插件映射实现可组合策略和闭环适配；3) 原子策略热重载消除策略更新停机时间。

Result: 在8块NVIDIA B300 GPU（NVLink连接）上评估显示：每次调优器决策仅产生80-130ns开销（小于集体通信延迟的0.03%），在加载时阻止所有测试的不安全插件行为，消息大小感知的eBPF策略在4-128 MiB范围内将AllReduce吞吐量提升最多27%。

Conclusion: NCCLbpf在保持高性能的同时提供了安全保障和热更新能力，为大规模分布式训练中的GPU集体通信提供了安全可扩展的插件化方案。

Abstract: NCCL is the de facto standard for collective GPU communication in large-scale distributed training, relying heavily on plugins to customize runtime behavior. However, these plugins execute as unverified native code within NCCL's address space, risking job crashes, silent state corruption, and downtime from restarts during policy updates. Inspired by kernel extensibility models, we introduce NCCLbpf, a verified, high-performance extension framework embedding a userspace eBPF runtime directly into NCCL's existing plugin interfaces, without modifying NCCL itself. NCCLbpf offers load-time static verification to prevent unsafe plugin execution, structured cross-plugin maps enabling composable policies and closed-loop adaptation, and atomic policy hot-reloads eliminating downtime previously required for policy updates. Evaluations on 8x NVIDIA B300 GPUs connected via NVLink demonstrate that NCCLbpf imposes just 80-130 ns overhead per tuner decision (less than 0.03% of collective latency), prevents all tested unsafe plugin behaviors at load-time, and enables a message-size-aware eBPF policy that improves AllReduce throughput by up to 27% over NCCL's default in the 4-128 MiB range.

</details>


### [2] [Subtime: Reversible Information Exchange and the Emergence of Classical Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11571)
*Paul L. Borrill*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出"亚时间"(subtime)概念——一种纠缠系统内可逆的信息交换模式——并证明经典时间作为退相干的渐近极限而出现。基于光子钟模型，作者发展了Oreshkov-Costa-Brukner框架的扩展形式，引入完美信息反馈(PIF)作为可逆性的信息论实现，证明了闭合因果回路中互信息守恒，并提出可逆因果原则(RCP)。该框架统一了Wheeler-Feynman吸收体理论、Bennett可逆计算、Shannon通信理论和过程矩阵形式主义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一量子引力、信息论与时间箭头问题，解释经典时间从量子可逆过程中涌现的机制，并解决因果性和可逆性之间的深层联系。

Method: 使用Oreshkov-Costa-Brukbner因果框架，引入显式时间反演对偶性条件，通过光子钟模型(单光子限制在两理想镜面间)作为具体物理实现，发展过程论形式化。

Result: 证明闭合因果回路中互信息守恒；熵量化未反射因果性的程度；识别了可逆数字链路和量子开关实验中的可访问实验特征；时间箭头被解释为"不完美因果回声"。

Conclusion: 时间箭头源于因果关系未能完美反射时的退相干；可逆因果原则(RCP)统一了Wheeler-Feynman吸收体理论、Bennett可逆计算、Shannon通信理论和过程矩阵形式主义，为时间本质提供了新的信息论视角。

Abstract: We formalize the concept of subtime -- a reversible mode of information interchange within entangled systems -- and show how classical time emerges as an asymptotic limit through decoherence. Building on the photon clock model, in which a single photon confined between two ideal mirrors creates an alternating causality regime, we develop a process-theoretic formalization using the Oreshkov--Costa--Brukner framework extended with an explicit time-reversal duality condition. We introduce Perfect Information Feedback (PIF) as the information-theoretic realization of this reversibility, demonstrating that mutual information is conserved in any closed causal loop and that entropy quantifies the degree of unreflected causality. We define the Reversible Causal Principle (RCP): every causal relation possesses a conjugate dual, and entropy, energy dissipation, and the classical arrow of time appear only when these alternating components decohere or fail to reflect perfectly. The framework unifies Wheeler--Feynman absorber theory, Bennett's reversible computation, Shannon's communication theory, and the process matrix formalism under a single symmetry principle, and identifies experimentally accessible signatures in reversible digital links and quantum switch experiments. The arrow of time, in this picture, records the universe's imperfect causal echo.

</details>


### [3] [Beyond BFS: A Comparative Study of Rooted Spanning Tree Algorithms on GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11645)
*Abhijeet Sahu,Srikar Vilas Donur*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了GPU上根生成树(RST)构建的替代策略，提出了PR-RST的GPU适配版本和GConn+Eulerian tour集成方法，后者在高直径图上比优化BFS快达300倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GPU上的RST构建依赖BFS，但BFS的O(D)步骤复杂度限制了高直径和幂律图上的并行性能，需要探索更高效的替代方案。

Method: 1)提出GPU适配的Path Reversal RST(PR-RST)算法，优化pointer-jumping和broadcast操作；2)评估GConn连通性框架与Eulerian tour结合的集成方法。

Result: 在10多个真实图上，GConn方法在高清度图上实现最高300倍加速，证明了O(log n)步骤复杂度的连通性方法可超越BFS。

Conclusion: O(log n)步骤复杂度的连通性方法在现代GPU硬件上的优势可弥补其结构开销，为GPU图分析中的RST构建提供了新的设计思路。

Abstract: Rooted spanning trees (RSTs) are a core primitive in parallel graph analytics, underpinning algorithms such as biconnected components and planarity testing. On GPUs, RST construction has traditionally relied on breadth-first search (BFS) due to its simplicity and work efficiency. However, BFS incurs an O(D) step complexity, which severely limits parallelism on high-diameter and power-law graphs. We present a comparative study of alternative RST construction strategies on modern GPUs. We introduce a GPU adaptation of the Path Reversal RST (PR-RST) algorithm, optimizing its pointer-jumping and broadcast operations for modern GPU architecture. In addition, we evaluate an integrated approach that combines a state-of-the-art connectivity framework (GConn) with Eulerian tour-based rooting. Across more than 10 real-world graphs, our results show that the GConn-based approach achieves up to 300x speedup over optimized BFS on high-diameter graphs. These findings indicate that the O(log n) step complexity of connectivity-based methods can outweigh their structural overhead on modern hardware, motivating a rethinking of RST construction in GPU graph analytics.

</details>


### [4] [Decentralized Orchestration Architecture for Fluid Computing: A Secure Distributed AI Use Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12001)
*Diego Cajaraville-Aboy,Ana Fernández-Vilas,Rebeca P. Díaz-Redondo,Manuel Fernández-Veiga,Pablo Picallo-López*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向流体计算环境的无知 Agnostic 多域编排架构，支持去中心化协调和意图驱动的部署，并针对多域去中心化联邦学习（DFL）场景设计了FU-HST异常检测机制以增强拜占庭安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式AI和IoT应用日益跨越异构资源（端设备、边缘/雾基础设施、云平台）并在不同管理域下执行，现有解决方案largely centralized且未明确考虑多域场景，需要一种能够将跨域资源统一管理同时保持各域本地自治的编排架构。

Method: 提出无知多域编排架构，通过域侧控制服务实现去中心化域间协调，使各域保持本地自治的同时共同实现租户的意图驱动部署请求；以多域DFL为代表性用例，引入FU-HST（SDN-enabled multi-domain anomaly detection）机制补充拜占庭鲁棒聚合；通过仿真在单域和多域设置下验证异常检测、DFL性能及计算/通信开销。

Result: 仿真验证了所提方法在单域和多域场景下的有效性，包括异常检测准确性、DFL性能以及可接受的计算/通信开销。

Conclusion: 所提无知多域编排架构能够有效支持流体计算环境下的多域资源协调，FU-HST机制可增强多域DFL在拜占庭威胁下的安全性，为跨域分布式系统的安全部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Distributed AI and IoT applications increasingly execute across heterogeneous resources spanning end devices, edge/fog infrastructure, and cloud platforms, often under different administrative domains. Fluid Computing has emerged as a promising paradigm for enhancing massive resource management across the computing continuum by treating such resources as a unified fabric, enabling optimal service-agnostic deployments driven by application requirements. However, existing solutions remain largely centralized and often do not explicitly address multi-domain considerations. This paper proposes an agnostic multi-domain orchestration architecture for fluid computing environments. The orchestration plane enables decentralized coordination among domains that maintain local autonomy while jointly realizing intent-based deployment requests from tenants, ensuring end-to-end placement and execution. To this end, the architecture elevates domain-side control services as first-class capabilities to support application-level enhancement at runtime. As a representative use case, we consider a multi-domain Decentralized Federated Learning (DFL) deployment under Byzantine threats. We leverage domain-side capabilities to enhance Byzantine security by introducing FU-HST, an SDN-enabled multi-domain anomaly detection mechanism that complements Byzantine-robust aggregation. We validate the approach via simulation in single- and multi-domain settings, evaluating anomaly detection, DFL performance, and computation/communication overhead.

</details>


### [5] [WORKSWORLD: A Domain for Integrated Numeric Planning and Scheduling of Distributed Pipelined Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12214)
*Taylor Paul,William Regli*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: This work pursues automated planning and scheduling of distributed data pipelines, or workflows. We develop a general workflow and resource graph representation that includes both data processing and sharing components with corresponding network interfaces for scheduling. Leveraging these graphs, we introduce WORKSWORLD, a new domain for numeric domain-independent planners designed for permanently scheduled workflows, like ingest pipelines. Our framework permits users to define data sources, available workflow components, and desired data destinations and formats without explicitly declaring the entire workflow graph as a goal. The planner solves a joint planning and scheduling problem, producing a plan that both builds the workflow graph and schedules its components on the resource graph. We empirically show that a state-of-the-art numeric planner running on commodity hardware with one hour of CPU time and 30GB of memory can solve linear-chain workflows of up to 14 components across eight sites.

</details>

### [6] [Context Before Code: An Experience Report on Vibe Coding in Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11073)
*Md Nasir Uddin Shuvo,Md Aidul Islam,Md Mahade Hasan,Muhammad Waseem,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 一篇关于对话式代码生成工具(vibe coding)在生产环境中应用的经验报告，发现生成代码在隔离规则和约束规范方面往往不足，需要人工进行架构设计和约束审计


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码生成工具在软件开发中日益普及，但关于生产约束下对话式"氛围编程"(vibe coding)的经验报告仍然有限

Method: 经验报告方式，通过两个实际系统构建：(1)多项目智能体学习平台，支持结构化内存和后台处理的项目级隔离；(2)学术RAG系统，提供引用支撑的答案、基于角色的访问控制和评估追踪

Result: 氛围编程加速了脚手架和集成工作；但生成的代码往往对隔离规则和基础设施约束指定不足，需要显式定义多租户、访问控制、内存策略、异步处理等方面的架构设计和验证

Conclusion: 工程努力从样板代码实现转向约束规范制定与执行审计；对话式代码生成在多租户、访问控制、内存策略、异步处理等"非委托区域"仍不足以满足生产可靠性需求

Abstract: Code-generating tools are increasingly used in software development, yet experience reports on conversational "vibe coding" under production constraints remain limited. This paper presents an experience report from a small full-stack team that applied contextual prompting and explicit architectural constraints to build (i) a multi-project agent learning platform designed for sustained, production-oriented use and (ii) an academic retrieval-augmented generation system.
  The agent platform supports multiple isolated projects, each with structured memory and background processing, thereby enforcing project-level isolation. The RAG system provides citation-grounded answers, role-based access control, and evaluation tracking.
  Across both systems, vibe coding accelerated scaffolding and integration. However, the generated code often under-specified isolation rules and infrastructure constraints when these were not explicitly defined. Consequently, aspects such as multi-tenancy, access control, memory policies, and asynchronous processing required deliberate architectural design and verification. We observe a shift in engineering effort from boilerplate implementation toward constraint specification and enforcement auditing. We also identify recurring architectural "non-delegation zones" where conversational code generation remains insufficient for production reliability.

</details>


### [7] [Quality-Driven Agentic Reasoning for LLM-Assisted Software Design: Questions-of-Thoughts (QoT) as a Time-Series Self-QA Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11082)
*Yen-Ku Liu,Yun-Cheng Tsai*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: QoT (Questions-of-Thoughts) 是一种质量驱动的推理时支架框架，通过将用户目标分解为有序的工程步骤序列和逐步自我验证问题来改进 LLM 生成的软件系统质量，在 API 设计、数据通信和文件系统三个后端工程领域评估，显示对更大模型和更复杂领域有一致性质量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 LLM 辅助软件开发面临实际部署障碍，包括实现不完整、模块化薄弱和安全实践不一致等问题，需要一种推理时的质量保证方法来改善生成系统的质量。

Method: 提出 QoT 框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 将用户目标转化为有序的工程步骤序列；(2) 逐步自我提问以验证约束条件并减少遗漏错误。评估使用 ISO/IEC 启发的质量评分标准，涵盖可扩展性、完整性、模块化和安全性四个维度，在 API 设计、数据通信和文件系统三个代表性后端工程领域进行测试。

Result: QoT 带来与模型容量相关的改进：较大模型和更复杂领域获得一致的质量提升，而较小模型在紧张上下文和规划预算下可能表现出权衡取舍。质量改进以 QoT 分数减去 NoQoT 分数的增量表示。

Conclusion: QoT 作为一种轻量级推理记录方法，能够稳定后续设计决策，为 LLM 辅助软件工程提供了一种可行的质量改进方案。研究成果包括开源的提示词、评分指南、原始生成结果和可复现实验的脚本。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have accelerated AI-assisted software development, yet practical deployment remains constrained by incomplete implementations, weak modularization, and inconsistent security practices. We introduce Questions-of-Thoughts (QoT), a quality-driven inference-time scaffold that turns a user goal into (i) an ordered sequence of engineering steps and (ii) stepwise self-questioning to verify constraints and reduce omission errors, while maintaining a lightweight reasoning record that stabilizes subsequent design decisions.
  We evaluate QoT across three representative backend engineering domains: API Design, Data Communication, and File Systems. Each task requires multi-module decomposition and exposes standard failure modes in LLM-generated systems. To enable data-driven comparison, we score generated artifacts using an ISO/IEC-inspired quality rubric that measures Scalability, Completeness, Modularity, and Security. We report domain-wise gains as the change in total quality score, defined as the QoT score minus the NoQoT score. Results show capacity-dependent improvements: QoT yields consistent quality improvements for larger models and more complex domains, while smaller models may exhibit trade-offs under tight context and planning budgets.
  We release an open artifact with prompts, scoring guidelines, raw generations, and scripts that reproduce the reported tables and figures to support applied AI and data analytics research.

</details>


### [8] [Type-safe Monitoring of Parameterized Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11104)
*Jan Baumeister,Bernd Finkbeiner,Florian Kohn*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文将参数化流集成到RTLola流监控框架中，处理无界数据域。证明了运行时错误消除的不可判定性，但通过精化类型系统可有效保证内存安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监控器是安全关键组件，必须确保无运行时错误。当监控器需要处理无界数据域（如无限数量的空域参与者）时，提供此类保证尤其具有挑战性，因为需要使用动态数据结构。

Method: 将参数化流集成到RTLola监控框架中，设计了精化类型系统来保证内存安全。

Result: 证明了运行时错误的消除在一般情况下是不可判定的，但可以通过精化类型系统有效确保。该类型分析在包括自主飞机监控规范在内的各种基准测试规范上表现良好。

Conclusion: 通过精化类型系统确保所有内存引用要么成功要么有默认值支持，从而有效保证运行时错误的缺失。类型分析在无人机监控规范等基准测试中表现良好。

Abstract: Stream-based monitoring is a real-time safety assurance mechanism for complex cyber-physical systems such as unmanned aerial vehicles. The monitor aggregates streams of input data from sensors and other sources to give real-time statistics and assessments of the system's health. Since the monitor is a safety-critical component, it is mandatory to ensure the absence of runtime errors in the monitor. Providing such guarantees is particularly challenging when the monitor must handle unbounded data domains, like an unlimited number of airspace participants, requiring the use of dynamic data structures. This paper provides a type-safe integration of parameterized streams into the stream-based monitoring framework RTLola. Parameterized streams generalize individual streams to sets of an unbounded number of stream instances and provide a systematic mechanism for memory management. We show that the absence of runtime errors is, in general, undecidable but can be effectively ensured with a refinement type system that guarantees all memory references are either successful or backed by a default value. We report on the performance of the type analysis on example specifications from a range of benchmarks, including specifications from the monitoring of autonomous aircraft.

</details>


### [9] [Bridging Behavioral Biometrics and Source Code Stylometry: A Survey of Programmer Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11150)
*Marek Horvath,Emilia Pietrikova,Diomidis Spinellis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 一篇关于程序员归因研究的系统性映射研究，从135篇候选文献中筛选出47篇（2012-2025年），分析其作者识别任务、特征类别、学习建模方法、数据集来源和评估实践，并提出分类法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序员归因问题在软件工程、安全和数字取证领域被广泛研究，产生了大量方法多样的研究成果，但缺乏系统性的综合整理。该研究旨在通过系统性映射研究来统一理解该领域的研究现状和方法论趋势。

Method: 系统性映射研究：从135篇候选文献中通过结构化筛选流程选取47篇研究，分析维度包括作者识别任务、特征类别、学习建模方法、数据集来源、评估实践，并推导出分类法，进行内容层面的主题聚类分析。

Result: 研究表明该领域强调查闭环境下的作者归因，使用风格计量特征，高度依赖少量基准数据集；行为信号、作者验证和可重复性仍是较少探索的领域。

Conclusion: 将现有研究整合为统一框架，识别出方法论方面的研究缺口，为未来研究提供指导方向。

Abstract: Programmer attribution seeks to identify or verify the author of a source code artifact using stylistic, structural, or behavioural characteristics. This problem has been studied across software engineering, security, and digital forensics, resulting in a growing and methodologically diverse set of publications. This paper presents a systematic mapping study of programmer attribution research focused on source code analysis. From an initial set of 135 candidate publications, 47 studies published between 2012 and 2025 were selected through a structured screening process. The included works are analysed along several dimensions, including authorship tasks, feature categories, learning and modelling approaches, dataset sources, and evaluation practices. Based on this analysis, we derive a taxonomy that relates stylistic and behavioural feature types to commonly used machine learning techniques and provide a descriptive overview of publication trends, benchmarks, programming languages. A content-level analysis highlights the main thematic clusters in the field. The results indicate a strong focus on closed-world authorship attribution using stylometric features and a heavy reliance on a small number of benchmark datasets, while behavioural signals, authorship verification, and reproducibility remain less explored. The study consolidates existing research into a unified framework and outlines methodological gaps that can guide future work. This manuscript is currently under review. The present version is a preprint.

</details>


### [10] [ExecVerify: White-Box RL with Verifiable Stepwise Rewards for Code Execution Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11226)
*Lingxiao Tang,He Ye,Zhaoyang Chu,Muyang Ye,Zhongxin Liu,Xiaoxue Ren,Lingfeng Bao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ExecVerify是一种通过强化学习和可验证白盒奖励提升代码LLM执行推理的方法，无需依赖教师解释即可直接验证中间执行步骤，通过两阶段训练管道实现7B模型性能接近32B模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Code LLMs struggle with code execution reasoning, especially smaller models. Existing SFT methods rely on teacher-generated explanations (I/O prediction chains and natural-language execution trace descriptions), but cannot explicitly verify intermediate execution steps during training, leading to mere explanation matching. Additionally, training data lacks explicit difficulty control.

Method: 1. 构建多难度级别的数据集（通过约束程序合成）2. 应用强化学习（RL）奖励来自执行轨迹的可验证白盒奖励，包括下一语句预测和变量值/类型预测 3. 采用两阶段训练管道：先增强执行推理，再迁移到代码生成

Result: 7B模型在代码推理基准测试中达到与32B模型相当的性能，在代码生成任务上比强后训练基线提高pass@1最多5.9%

Conclusion: ExecVerify通过将训练目标与每个执行步骤的语义正确性对齐，有效解决了代码执行推理问题。该方法结合可验证的白盒奖励和多难度数据集训练，显著提升了小规模模型的代码推理和生成能力。

Abstract: Code LLMs still struggle with code execution reasoning, especially in smaller models. Existing methods rely on supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with teacher-generated explanations, primarily in two forms: (1) input-output (I/O) prediction chains and (2) natural-language descriptions of execution traces. However, intermediate execution steps cannot be explicitly verified during SFT, so the training objective can reduce to merely matching teacher explanations. Moreover, training data is typically collected without explicit control over task difficulty. We introduce ExecVerify, which goes beyond text imitation by incorporating verifiable white-box rewards derived from execution traces, including next-statement prediction and variable value/type prediction. Our work first builds a dataset with multiple difficulty levels via constraint-based program synthesis. Then, we apply reinforcement learning (RL) to reward correct answers about both intermediate execution steps and final outputs, aligning the training objective with semantic correctness at each execution step. Finally, we adopt a two-stage training pipeline that first enhances execution reasoning and then transfers to code generation. Experiments demonstrate that a 7B model trained with ExecVerify achieves performance comparable to 32B models on code reasoning benchmarks and improves pass@1 by up to 5.9\% on code generation tasks over strong post-training baselines.

</details>


### [11] [Unveiling Practical Shortcomings of Patch Overfitting Detection Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11262)
*David Williams,Ioakim Avraam,Aldeida Aleti,Matias Martinez,Justyna Petke,Federica Sarro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究对六种先进的补丁过拟合检测（POD）方法进行了首次综合基准测试，发现简单的随机选择实际上优于大多数现有POD工具（71%-96%的情况），表明当前POD工具的实际价值有限，需要在真实数据上进行基准测试以证明其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的补丁正确性评估（过拟合检测）技术通常是孤立评估的，且使用的数据集不能反映APR工具在实际使用中生成的正确补丁到过拟合补丁的分布，因此仍不清楚这些技术在实际中的有效性。

Method: 构建了反映真实假设的数据集（与实际APR工具运行条件相同产生的补丁）；使用该数据对6种先进的POD方法进行了基准测试，涵盖静态分析、动态测试和基于学习的方法；与两个随机采样基线进行对比（一个来自先前工作，一个为本文提出）。

Result: 简单的随机选择优于所有POD工具，比例为71%至96%（取决于POD工具）。

Conclusion: 当前POD工具的实际应用价值有限，需要开发新方法；任何POD工具必须在真实数据上并与随机采样进行对比才能证明其实际效果；作者呼吁APR社区继续改进POD技术并采用所提出的基准测试方法。

Abstract: Automated Program Repair (APR) can reduce the time developers spend debugging, allowing them to focus on other aspects of software development. Automatically generated bug patches are typically validated through software testing. However, this method can lead to patch overfitting, i.e., generating patches that pass the given tests but are still incorrect.
  Patch correctness assessment (also known as overfitting detection) techniques have been proposed to identify patches that overfit. However, prior work often assessed the effectiveness of these techniques in isolation and on datasets that do not reflect the distribution of correct-to-overfitting patches that would be generated by APR tools in typical use; thus, we still do not know their effectiveness in practice.
  This work presents the first comprehensive benchmarking study of several patch overfitting detection (POD) methods in a practical scenario. To this end, we curate datasets that reflect realistic assumptions (i.e., patches produced by tools run under the same experimental conditions). Next, we use these data to benchmark six state-of-the-art POD approaches -- spanning static analysis, dynamic testing, and learning-based approaches -- against two baselines based on random sampling (one from prior work and one proposed herein).
  Our results are striking: Simple random selection outperforms all POD tools for 71% to 96% of cases, depending on the POD tool. This suggests two main takeaways: (1) current POD tools offer limited practical benefit, highlighting the need for novel techniques; (2) any POD tool must be benchmarked on realistic data and against random sampling to prove its practical effectiveness. To this end, we encourage the APR community to continue improving POD techniques and to adopt our proposed methodology for practical benchmarking; we make our data and code available to facilitate such adoption.

</details>


### [12] [Enhancing Requirements Traceability Link Recovery: A Novel Approach with T-SimCSE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11800)
*Ye Wang,Wenqing Wang,Kun Hu,Qiao Huang,Liping Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于SimCSE的需求追踪链接恢复方法T-SimCSE，该方法无需标注数据即可计算需求与目标构件的相似度，并通过specificity指标重排序后建立追踪链接，在十个数据集上验证了其优于现有方法的召回率和MAP性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求追踪对于确保软件质量和响应需求变更至关重要，但现有基于预训练语言模型的方法恢复的追踪链接不够准确，且多数方法需要大量标注数据，而目前公开的标注数据集非常有限。

Method: T-SimCSE首先使用SimCSE模型计算需求与目标构件之间的相似度，并采用新指标(specificity)对目标构件进行重排序，最后选择top-K个目标构件与需求建立追踪链接。

Result: 在十个公开数据集上的评估结果表明，T-SimCSE在召回率和平均精度均值(MAP)方面均取得了最优性能。

Conclusion: T-SimCSE在召回率(Recall)和平均精度均值(MAP)方面优于其他对比方法，验证了基于SimCSE的需求可追踪性链接恢复方法的有效性。

Abstract: Requirements traceability plays an important role in ensuring software quality and responding to changes in requirements. Requirements trace links (such as the links between requirements and other software artifacts) underpin the modeling and implementation of requirements traceability. With the rapid development of artificial intelligence, more and more pre-trained language models (PLMs) techniques are applied to the automatic recovery of requirements trace links. However, the requirements traceability links recovered by these approaches are not accurate enough, and many approaches require a large labeled dataset for training. Currently, there are very few labeled datasets available. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a novel requirements traceability link recovery approach called T-SimCSE, which is based on a PLM -- SimCSE. SimCSE has the advantages of not requiring labeled data, having broad applicability, and performing well. T-SimCSE firstly uses the SimCSE model to calculate the similarity between requirements and target artifacts, and employs a new metric (i.e. specificity) to reorder those target artifacts. Finally, the trace links are created between the requirement and the top-K target artifacts. We have evaluated T-SimCSE on ten public datasets by comparing them with other approaches. The results show that T-SimCSE achieves superior performance in terms of recall and Mean Average Precision (MAP).

</details>


### [13] [QUARE: Multi-Agent Negotiation for Balancing Quality Attributes in Requirements Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11890)
*Haowei Cheng,Milhan Kim,Foutse Khomh,Teeradaj Racharak,Nobukazu Yoshioka,Naoyasu Ubayashi,Hironori Washizaki*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: QUARE是一个多Agent需求工程框架，通过五个质量专业Agent的结构化协商来解决跨质量冲突，实现98.2%合规覆盖率和94.9%语义保真度，表明有效的RE自动化取决于架构分解和交互协议而非模型规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求工程(RE)对软件成功至关重要，但自动化仍具挑战性，因为需要平衡多个经常冲突的质量属性同时保持利益相关者意图。现有的大型语言模型(LLM)方法主要依赖单体推理或隐式聚合，缺乏系统性地暴露和解决跨质量冲突的能力。

Method: 提出了QUARE框架，包含：1) 五质量专业Agent（安全、效率、绿色、可信、负责）的结构化协商协议；2) 迭代提议-批评-合成机制解决跨质量冲突；3) 拓扑验证将协商结果转换为结构合理的KAOS目标模型；4) 检索增强生成(RAG)验证是否符合行业标准。

Result: 在5个案例研究（来自MARE、iReDev基准和工业自动驾驶规范）上评估，结果显示：合规覆盖率98.2%（比两个基线提高105%），语义保真度94.9%（比最佳基线提高2.3个百分点），可验证性高（4.96/5.0），生成的需求比现有多Agent RE框架多25-43%。

Conclusion: QUARE通过多Agent架构和结构化协商协议有效解决了需求工程中的跨质量冲突问题。实验结果表明，该方法在合规覆盖率达98.2%、语义保真度达94.9%、可验证性达4.96/5.0，生成的需求数量比现有方法多25-43%。研究表明，有效的RE自动化更多取决于原则性的架构分解、明确的交互协议和自动验证，而非模型规模。

Abstract: Requirements engineering (RE) is critical to software success, yet automating it remains challenging because multiple, often conflicting quality attributes must be balanced while preserving stakeholder intent. Existing Large-Language-Model (LLM) approaches predominantly rely on monolithic reasoning or implicit aggregation, limiting their ability to systematically surface and resolve cross-quality conflicts. We present QUARE (Quality-Aware Requirements Engineering), a multi-agent framework that formulates requirements analysis as structured negotiation among five quality-specialized agents (Safety, Efficiency, Green, Trustworthiness, and Responsibility), coordinated by a dedicated orchestrator. QUARE introduces a dialectical negotiation protocol that explicitly exposes inter-quality conflicts and resolves them through iterative proposal, critique, and synthesis. Negotiated outcomes are transformed into structurally sound KAOS goal models via topology validation and verified against industry standards through retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). We evaluate QUARE on five case studies drawn from established RE benchmarks (MARE, iReDev) and an industrial autonomous-driving specification, spanning safety-critical, financial, and information-system domains. Results show that QUARE achieves 98.2% compliance coverage (+105% over both baselines), 94.9% semantic preservation (+2.3 percentage points over the best baseline), and high verifiability (4.96/5.0), while generating 25-43% more requirements than existing multi-agent RE frameworks. These findings suggest that effective RE automation depends less on model scale than on principled architectural decomposition, explicit interaction protocols, and automated verification.

</details>


### [14] [A Collaborative and Pattern-Based Training Approach to Knowledge Acquisition and Decision-Making During the Design of Software Architectures Courses: A Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11904)
*Wilson Libardo Pantoja Yepez,Luis Mariano Bibbo,Julio Ariel Hurtado Alegría*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 描述了哥伦比亚UNICAUCA与阿根廷UNPL之间的软件架构协作学习项目，通过比较课程、实施培训模式、进行跨国团队开发迭代来评估培训效果，提出培训模式目录以解决软件架构教育中的抽象性、真实环境重现等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决软件架构教育中面临的挑战：架构的抽象性、先决知识要求、难以重现真实项目环境、团队协作挑战和资源限制。目标是应用和评估培训模式，识别可在其他情境中复制的有效实践。

Method: 两所大学比较了学习目标、课程和教学策略以找到共同点；实施选定的培训模式并测量对教授和学生的影响；在课程最后阶段进行全球开发体验，合并两校团队进行开发迭代；基于技术接受模型(TAM)评估所达到的能力、感知有用性和易用性。

Result: 成功实施了两校间的协作学习模式，开发了培训模式目录，解决了行业对软件架构设计技能的需求。基于TAM的评估表明培训模式具有良好的感知有用性和易用性，学生通过全球开发体验获得了实际的架构决策能力。

Conclusion: 提出了一个培训模式目录，帮助模拟类行业环境并为增量学习构建架构知识。架构决策能力需要通过时间和多个项目经验来发展，强调了实用、结构化培训项目的必要性。研究表明协作学习模式在软件架构教育中具有复制和推广的价值。

Abstract: This article describes a collaborative learning experience on Software Architecture (SA) between Universidad del Cauca (UNICAUCA) in Colombia and Universidad Nacional de la Plata (UNPL) in Argentina. The goal was to apply and evaluate training patterns, identifying effective practices for replication in other contexts. During the planning phase, both universities compared learning objectives, curricula, and teaching strategies to find common ground for improving student training. Selected training patterns were implemented, and their impact on professors and students was measured. As an integrating activity, a global development experience was carried out in the final part of the course, merging the work teams of the two educational institutions in a development iteration. The evaluation of this experience focused on the competencies achieved through the training patterns, their perceived usefulness, and ease of use based on the Technology Acceptance Model (TAM). The training addressed industry needs for software architecture design skills despite challenges such as the abstract nature of architectures, prerequisite knowledge, difficulty in recreating realistic project environments, team collaboration challenges, and resource limitations. A catalog of training patterns was proposed to provide quality training. These patterns help simulate industry-like environments and structure architectural knowledge for incremental learning. The ability to make architectural decisions is developed over time and through multiple project experiences, emphasizing the need for practical, well-structured training programs.

</details>

### [15] [BEACON: Budget-Aware Entity Matching Across Domains (Extended Technical Report)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11391)
*Nicholas Pulsone,Roee Shraga,Gregory Goren*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: BEACON是一个针对低资源跨域实体匹配(EM)的分布感知、预算感知框架，通过嵌入表示引导out-of-domain样本选择，在有限标注数据条件下提升EM性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习虽提升了实体匹配性能，但微调通常需要大量标注数据，而标注数据获取成本高耗时长。在电商匹配场景中，不同领域的标注稀缺程度差异很大，因此如何利用有限标注数据训练准确的领域特定EM模型成为关键问题。

Method: 提出BEACON框架，利用成对候选匹配的嵌入表示来指导有限in-domain监督下out-of-domain样本的有效选择。框架具有distribution-aware和budget-aware特性。

Result: 在多个来自EM基准测试的领域划分数据集上进行的广泛实验表明，BEACON在不同训练预算下 consistently outperforms state-of-the-art方法。

Conclusion: BEACON是一个有效的低资源跨域实体匹配框架，通过嵌入表示引导out-of-domain样本选择，在不同训练预算下均优于现有最先进方法。

Abstract: Entity Matching (EM)--the task of determining whether two data records refer to the same real-world entity--is a core task in data integration. Recent advances in deep learning have set a new standard for EM, particularly through fine-tuning Pretrained Language Models (PLMs) and, more recently, Large Language Models (LLMs). However, fine-tuning typically requires large amounts of labeled data, which are expensive and time-consuming to obtain. In the context of e-commerce matching, labeling scarcity varies widely across domains, raising the question of how to intelligently train accurate domain-specific EM models with limited labeled data. In this work we assume users have only limited amount of labels for a specific target domain but have access to labeled data from other domains. We introduce BEACON, a distribution-aware, budget-aware framework for low-resource EM across domains. BEACON leverages the insight that embedding representations of pairwise candidate matches can guide the effective selection of out-of-domain samples under limited in-domain supervision. We conduct extensive experiments across multiple domain-partitioned datasets derived from established EM benchmarks, demonstrating that BEACON consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods under different training budgets.

</details>


### [16] [Faster Relational Algorithms Using Geometric Data Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11402)
*Aryan Esmailpour,Stavros Sintos*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出RBBD树框架，通过几何洞察力设计针对关系型数据的更快优化算法，利用高效采样和计数技术实现RBBD树的即时扩展，避免物化连接结果。作为应用，提出关系k-center/means/median聚类算法，在保持相同近似保证的前提下，运行时间比现有技术提高k倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系数据上的优化任务（如聚类）需要执行代价高昂的连接操作才能访问完整数据集。虽然几何数据结构（如BBD树）在标准计算环境中能产生快速近似算法，但其应用于关系数据仍不明确，因为连接输出的规模过大。

Method: 开发了RBBD树（随机BBD树），专为关系场景设计。不需要完全构建RBBD树，而是通过利用关系连接上的高效采样和计数技术，实现RBBD树的即时高效扩展，仅维护必要的部分，从而能够在不物化连接结果的情况下模拟几何查询程序。

Result: 以关系k-center/means/median聚类为例，提出算法在保持相同近似保证的前提下，将运行时间比现有技术提高k倍。该方法具有通用性，可应用于关系设置中的各种优化问题。

Conclusion: 通过将几何洞察力与关系数据处理相结合，提出了一种新的算法框架RBBD树，成功解决了关系数据上几何优化算法的可扩展性问题，为关系型聚类及相关优化问题提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Optimization tasks over relational data, such as clustering, often suffer from the prohibitive cost of join operations, which are necessary to access the full dataset. While geometric data structures like BBD trees yield fast approximation algorithms in the standard computational setting, their application to relational data remains unclear due to the size of the join output. In this paper, we introduce a framework that leverages geometric insights to design faster algorithms when the data is stored as the results of a join query in a relational database. Our core contribution is the development of the RBBD tree, a randomized variant of the BBD tree tailored for relational settings. Instead of completely constructing the RBBD tree, by leveraging efficient sampling and counting techniques over relational joins, we enable on-the-fly efficient expansion of the RBBD tree, maintaining only the necessary parts. This allows us to simulate geometric query procedures without materializing the join result. As an application, we present algorithms that improve the state-of-the-art for relational $k$-center/means/median clustering by a factor of $k$ in running time while maintaining the same approximation guarantees. Our method is general and can be applied to various optimization problems in the relational setting.

</details>


### [17] [LHGstore: An In-Memory Learned Graph Storage for Fast Updates and Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11596)
*Pengpeng Qiao,Zhiwei Zhang,Xinzhou Wang,Zhetao Li,Xiaochun Cao,Yang Cao*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: LHGstore是一种面向现代多核CPU的度感知学习型层次图存储系统，首次将学习索引集成到图管理中。该系统采用两级层次结构解耦顶点和边访问，并为不同度的顶点使用自适应数据结构（低度顶点使用轻量数组以最大化遍历局部性，高度顶点使用学习索引以提高更新吞吐量）。实验表明，LHGstore相比SOTA内存图存储系统实现5.9-28.2倍吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用依赖内存动态图，需要高效处理频繁更新同时支持低延迟分析。由于更新效率与遍历局部性之间的权衡，特别是在高度偏斜的度分布下，同时实现这两个目标仍然具有挑战性。这促使设计针对现代多核CPU优化的图索引方案。

Method: 设计了一种度感知的学习型层次图存储（LHGstore），首次将学习索引集成到图管理中。该系统采用两级层次结构解耦顶点和边访问，并根据顶点度自适应组织边数据结构：低度顶点使用轻量数组最大化遍历局部性，高度顶点应用学习索引提高更新吞吐量。

Result: 实验表明，LHGstore实现了5.9-28.2倍更高的吞吐量，显著优于SOTA内存图存储系统。

Conclusion: 通过将学习索引与图存储管理相结合，LHGstore成功解决了动态图更新效率与遍历局部性之间的权衡问题，为内存图存储提供了一种新的优化方向。

Abstract: Various real-world applications rely on in-memory dynamic graphs that must efficiently handle frequent updates while supporting low-latency analytics on evolving structures. Achieving both objectives remains challenging due to the trade-off between update efficiency and traversal locality, particularly under highly skewed degree distributions. This motivates the design of graph indexing schemes optimized for in-memory graph management on modern multi-core CPUs. We present LHGstore, a degree-aware Learned Hierarchical Graph storage that, for the first time, integrates learned indexing into graph management. LHGstore designs a two-level hierarchy that decouples vertex and edge access and further organizes each vertex's edges using data structures adaptive to its degree. Lightweight arrays are used for low-degree vertices to maximize traversal locality, while learned indexes are applied to high-degree vertices to improve update throughput. Extensive experiments show that LHGstore achieves 5.9-28.2$\times$ higher throughput and significantly faster analytics than SOTA in-memory graph storage systems.

</details>


### [18] [Sema: A High-performance System for LLM-based Semantic Query Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11622)
*Kangkang Qi,Dongyang Xie,Wenbo Li,Hao Zhang,Yuanyuan Zhu,Jeffrey Xu Yu,Kangfei Zhao*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Sema是一个基于DuckDB的高性能语义查询引擎，将LLM驱动的语义操作符作为一等公民，支持在标准SQL中无缝注入自然语言表达式，实现端到端优化和执行。通过逻辑层面的自然语言压缩和关系约束推导，以及运行时采用自适应查询执行(AQE)动态优化，在20个语义查询上实现2-10倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统存在以下问题：1) DataFrame原语缺乏现代DBMS的高效执行基础设施；2) SQL UDF将语义逻辑与查询优化器隔离，使用户负担实现复杂性；3) LLM调用的高成本和不确定性使得传统优化规则和成本模型不适用。

Method: Sema构建在DuckDB之上，将LLM驱动的语义操作符作为一等公民。在逻辑层面，压缩自然语言表达式并从语义操作符推导关系约束；在运行时，采用自适应查询执行(AQE)动态重排序操作符、融合语义操作和应用提示批处理。

Result: 在20个语义查询（分类、摘要和提取任务）上进行评估，Sema相对于三个基线系统实现了2-10倍的加速，同时达到具有竞争力的结果质量。

Conclusion: Sema通过将LLM作为一等公民集成到数据库中，实现了2-10倍的加速，同时保持竞争力的结果质量。SemaSQL允许用户无缝地将自然语言表达式注入标准SQL子句，实现端到端优化和执行。在逻辑层面，压缩自然语言表达式并从语义操作符推断关系约束；在运行时，采用自适应查询执行(AQE)动态重排序操作符、融合语义操作和应用提示批处理，在精度约束下寻求token消耗和延迟之间的Pareto最优执行路径。

Abstract: The integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into data analytics has unlocked powerful capabilities for reasoning over bulk structured and unstructured data. However, existing systems typically rely on either DataFrame primitives, which lack the efficient execution infrastructure of modern DBMSs, or SQL User-Defined Functions (UDFs), which isolate semantic logic from the query optimizer and burden users with implementation complexities. The LLM-powered semantic operators also bring new challenges due to the high cost and non-deterministic nature of LLM invocation, where conventional optimization rules and cost models are inapplicable for their optimization.
  To bridge these gaps, we present Sema, a high-performance semantic query engine built on DuckDB that treats LLM-powered semantic operators as first-class citizens. Sema introduces SemaSQL, a declarative dialect that allows users seamlessly inject natural language expressions into standard SQL clauses, enabling end-to-end optimization and execution. At the logical level, the optimizer of Sema compresses natural language expressions and deduces relational constraints from semantic operators. At runtime, Sema employs Adaptive Query Execution (AQE) to dynamically reorder operators, fuse semantic operations, and apply prompt batching. This approach seeks a Pareto-optimal execution path balancing token consumption and latency under accuracy constraints. We evaluate Sema on 20 semantic queries across classification, summarization, and extraction tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that Sema achieves $2-10 \times$ speedup against three baseline systems while achieving competitive result quality.

</details>


### [19] [OMNIA: Closing the Loop by Leveraging LLMs for Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11820)
*Frédéric Ieng,Soror Sahri,Mourad Ouzzani,Massinissa Hammaz,Salima Benbernou,Hanieh Khorashadizadeh,Sven Groppe,Farah Benamara*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: OMNIA是一个两阶段知识图谱补全方法，通过聚类生成候选三元组，结合轻量级嵌入过滤和LLM语义验证来完成知识图谱补全，在不使用外部源的情况下显著提升F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前知识图谱自动构建尤其是使用LLM时，常产生不完整或嘈杂的输出。现有知识图谱补全方法存在局限性：基于结构嵌入的方法忽略语义，而语言模型方法则忽视图结构且依赖外部资源。

Method: 提出两阶段方法OMNIA：1）第一阶段通过对知识图谱内语义相关的实体和关系进行聚类来生成候选三元组；2）第二阶段通过轻量级嵌入过滤进行初步筛选，然后利用LLM进行语义验证。

Result: 在多个数据集上的广泛实验表明，OMNIA相比传统基于嵌入的模型显著提升了F1分数。聚类和过滤阶段有效降低了搜索空间和验证成本。

Conclusion: OMNIA在知识图谱补全任务上展现出良好的有效性和效率，能够在不依赖外部知识源的情况下，针对LLM生成图谱中最常见的隐含语义进行补全。

Abstract: Knowledge Graphs (KGs) are widely used to represent structured knowledge, yet their automatic construction, especially with Large Language Models (LLMs), often results in incomplete or noisy outputs. Knowledge Graph Completion (KGC) aims to infer and add missing triples, but most existing methods either rely on structural embeddings that overlook semantics or language models that ignore the graph's structure and depend on external sources. In this work, we present OMNIA, a two-stage approach that bridges structural and semantic reasoning for KGC. It first generates candidate triples by clustering semantically related entities and relations within the KG, then validates them through lightweight embedding filtering followed by LLM-based semantic validation. OMNIA performs on the internal KG, without external sources, and specifically targets implicit semantics that are most frequent in LLM-generated graphs. Extensive experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate that OMNIA significantly improves F1-score compared to traditional embedding-based models. These results highlight OMNIA's effectiveness and efficiency, as its clustering and filtering stages reduce both search space and validation cost while maintaining high-quality completion.

</details>


### [20] [Numerical benchmark for damage identification in Structural Health Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12069)
*Francesca Marafini,Giacomo Zini,Alberto Barontini,Nuno Mendes,Alice Cicirello,Michele Betti,Gianni Bartoli*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于结构健康监测(SHM)的开源模拟数据集，包含动态(加速度)和静态(位移)测量数据，旨在解决数据驱动和混合物理-数据方法验证中数据集匮乏的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前工程领域验证新型数据驱动策略和混合物理-数据方法时，缺乏可用于验证的数据集是最大挑战之一。数据所有权、安全性、访问权限和元数据便利性等问题阻碍了SHM等领域的进展。

Method: 以固定-固定钢梁为数值基准，采用单自由度(SDOF)系统生成加速度记录，欧拉-伯努利梁理论生成位移测量。数据生成过程纳入了环境与操作变化(EOVs)、不同类型损伤、测量噪声和传感器故障等因素，并使用并行计算技术。数据和代码均开源提供。

Result: 生成了一个综合的SHM模拟数据集，包含动态和静态测量值，并提供了详细的数据类型、格式和采集方法说明。数据集和源代码均已开源发布。

Conclusion: 该数据集为研究人员开发和完善SHM技术提供了宝贵的资源，通过提供可访问的高质量合成数据促进该领域的进步，确保了研究的可重复性和可复现性。

Abstract: The availability of a dataset for validation and verification purposes of novel data-driven strategies and/or hybrid physics-data approaches is currently one of the most pressing challenges in the engineering field. Data ownership, security, access and metadata handiness are currently hindering advances across many fields, particularly in Structural Health Monitoring (SHM) applications. This paper presents a simulated SHM dataset, comprised of dynamic and static measurements (i.e., acceleration and displacement), and includes the conceptual framework designed to generate it. The simulated measurements were generated to incorporate the effects of Environmental and Operational Variations (EOVs), different types of damage, measurement noise and sensor faults and malfunctions, in order to account for scenarios that may occur during real acquisitions. A fixed-fixed steel beam structure was chosen as reference for the numerical benchmark. The simulated monitoring was operated under the assumptions of a Single Degree of Freedom (SDOF) for generating acceleration records and of the Euler-Bernoulli beam for the simulated displacement measurements. The generation process involved the use of parallel computation, which is detailed within the provided open-source code. The generated data is also available open-source, thus ensuring reproducibility, repeatability and accessibility for further research. The comprehensive description of data types, formats, and collection methodologies makes this dataset a valuable resource for researchers aiming to develop or refine SHM techniques, fostering advancements in the field through accessible, high-quality synthetic data.

</details>


### [21] [Structure Selection for Fairness-Constrained Differentially Private Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12112)
*Naeim Ghahramanpour,Mostafa Milani*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出PrivCI方法，通过在差分隐私合成数据生成过程中强制执行条件独立性(CI)约束来解决公平性问题。该方法在测量阶段使用CI感知的贪婪MST算法，将可行性检查集成到指数机制下的Kruskal构造中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准差分隐私合成器保留数据中的所有依赖关系，包括敏感属性与结果之间的虚假相关性，这在公平性关键场景中会重现不需要的偏差。现有的DP合成方法没有考虑条件独立性约束，无法满足领域知识或法律要求。

Method: 提出PrivCI方法，在测量阶段通过CI感知的贪婪MST算法强制执行条件独立性约束。该算法将可行性检查集成到指数机制下的Kruskal构造中，通过两阶段过程：(i)Privatize选定的边缘分布(通常通过MST结构化)，(ii)拟合与噪声边缘分布一致的概率模型。

Result: 在标准公平性基准测试上的实验表明，PrivCI在满足指定CI约束的同时，实现了比基线方法更强的保真度和预测准确性。

Conclusion: PrivCI通过在差分隐私合成过程中有效集成条件独立性约束，为公平性关键场景中的安全数据发布提供了一种原则性的解决方案，平衡了数据效用和公平性要求。

Abstract: Differential privacy (DP) enables safe data release, with synthetic data generation emerging as a common approach in recent years. Yet standard synthesizers preserve all dependencies in the data, including spurious correlations between sensitive attributes and outcomes. In fairness-critical settings, this reproduces unwanted bias. A principled remedy is to enforce conditional independence (CI) constraints, which encode domain knowledge or legal requirements that outcomes be independent of sensitive attributes once admissible factors are accounted for. DP synthesis typically proceeds in two phases: (i) a measure- ment step that privatizes selected marginals, often structured via minimum spanning trees (MSTs), and (ii) a reconstruction step that fits a probabilistic model consistent with the noisy marginals. We propose PrivCI, which enforces CI during the measurement step via a CI-aware greedy MST algorithm that integrates feasibility checks into Kruskal's construction under the exponential mechanism, improving accuracy over competing methods. Experiments on standard fairness benchmarks show that PrivCI achieves stronger fidelity and predictive accuracy than prior baselines while satisfying the specified CI constraints.

</details>

### [22] [DIVE: Scaling Diversity in Agentic Task Synthesis for Generalizable Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11076)
*Aili Chen,Chi Zhang,Junteng Liu,Jiangjie Chen,Chengyu Du,Yunji Li,Ming Zhong,Qin Wang,Zhengmao Zhu,Jiayuan Song,Ke Ji,Junxian He,Pengyu Zhao,Yanghua Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DIVE方法，通过反转任务合成顺序（先执行真实工具再推导任务）来解决工具使用LLM的泛化脆弱性问题。该方法在两个可控维度上扩展结构多样性：工具池覆盖率和每任务工具集多样性。实验表明，在373个工具、5个领域的数据上训练Qwen3-8B，多样性扩展比数量扩展更能提升OOD泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前后训练工具使用LLM的工作虽然在合成任务方面取得进展，但在任务和工具集变化下的鲁棒泛化仍然存在挑战。作者将此脆弱性归因于合成任务的多样性不足。扩展多样性困难的原因在于：训练要求任务保持可执行和可验证，而泛化要求覆盖多样化的工具类型、工具集组合和异构的工具使用模式。

Method: 提出DIVE方法，包含两个可控的结构多样性扩展轴：（1）工具池覆盖率（tool-pool coverage）；（2）每任务工具集多样性（per-task toolset variety）。此外设计了"证据收集-任务推导"循环（Evidence Collection--Task Derivation loop），在5个领域的373个工具上诱导丰富的多步工具使用模式。该方法的核心创新在于反转传统合成顺序：先执行多样化的真实世界工具并生成执行轨迹，再从轨迹中严格推导 entailed tasks，确保任务的可执行性和可验证性。

Result: 在9个OOD基准测试中，Qwen3-8B模型（48k SFT + 3.2k RL训练）平均提升22个百分点，比最强8B基线提升68个百分点。控制变量 scaling 分析揭示：多样性扩展（diversity scaling）始终优于数量扩展（quantity scaling），即使使用少4倍的数据也能实现更好的OOD泛化。

Conclusion: DIVE通过反转任务合成顺序（先执行真实工具再推导任务）有效解决了工具使用LLM的泛化脆弱性问题。实验表明，在48k SFT + 3.2k RL数据上训练Qwen3-8B后，9个OOD基准测试平均提升22分，比最强8B基线提升68分。关键发现是多样性扩展优于数量扩展，即使数据量减少4倍也能获得更好的OOD泛化效果。

Abstract: Recent work synthesizes agentic tasks for post-training tool-using LLMs, yet robust generalization under shifts in tasks and toolsets remains an open challenge. We trace this brittleness to insufficient diversity in synthesized tasks. Scaling diversity is difficult because training requires tasks to remain executable and verifiable, while generalization demands coverage of diverse tool types, toolset combinations, and heterogeneous tool-use patterns. We propose DIVE, an evidence-driven recipe that inverts synthesis order, executing diverse, real-world tools first and reverse-deriving tasks strictly entailed by the resulting traces, thereby providing grounding by construction. DIVE scales structural diversity along two controllable axes, tool-pool coverage and per-task toolset variety, and an Evidence Collection--Task Derivation loop further induces rich multi-step tool-use patterns across 373 tools in five domains. Training Qwen3-8B on DIVE data (48k SFT + 3.2k RL) improves by +22 average points across 9 OOD benchmarks and outperforms the strongest 8B baseline by +68. Remarkably, controlled scaling analysis reveals that diversity scaling consistently outperforms quantity scaling for OOD generalization, even with 4x less data.

</details>


### [23] [A Survey of Reasoning in Autonomous Driving Systems: Open Challenges and Emerging Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11093)
*Kejin Yu,Yuhan Sun,Taiqiang Wu,Ruixu Zhang,Zhiqiang Lin,Yuxin Meng,Junjie Wang,Yujiu Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是首个系统综述将大语言模型和多模态模型集成到高等级自动驾驶的推理能力建设中。论文提出认知层次框架分解驾驶任务，识别七个核心推理挑战，从系统架构和评估方法两个视角综述前沿进展，并指出LLM推理的延迟特性与车辆控制毫秒级实时性要求之间存在根本矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1) 高等级自动驾驶的主要瓶颈正从感知局限性转向推理能力不足，缺乏鲁棒和可泛化的推理能力；2) 现有AD系统在长尾场景和需要人类判断的复杂社会交互中表现不佳；3) 大型语言模型和多模态模型的出现为将强大认知引擎集成到AD系统提供了变革性机会；4) 目前严重缺乏指导这种集成的系统性框架。

Method: 1) 提出一种新颖的认知层次框架（Cognitive Hierarchy），根据认知和交互复杂度分解单一的驾驶任务；2) 系统化地导出七个核心推理挑战；3) 从两个视角进行综述——系统中心法（智能体架构设计）和评估中心法（验证实践）；4) 分析了向整体化和可解释的"玻璃盒"智能体的发展趋势。

Result: 该综述揭示了明显的发展趋势：向整体化和可解释的"玻璃盒"智能体方向发展。同时识别出一个 fundamental and unresolved tension（基础性未解决的矛盾）：LLM推理的高延迟/deliberative特性与车辆控制的毫秒级安全关键需求之间的根本矛盾。

Conclusion: 提出一个基础性且未解决的核心矛盾：基于LLM的推理具有高延迟和 deliberative（审慎思考）特性，而车辆控制要求毫秒级响应且涉及安全关键决策。未来工作主要方向包括：开发可验证的神经符号架构、不确定性下的鲁棒推理，以及可扩展的隐式社会协商模型，以弥合符号到物理的鸿沟。

Abstract: The development of high-level autonomous driving (AD) is shifting from perception-centric limitations to a more fundamental bottleneck, namely, a deficit in robust and generalizable reasoning. Although current AD systems manage structured environments, they consistently falter in long-tail scenarios and complex social interactions that require human-like judgment. Meanwhile, the advent of large language and multimodal models (LLMs and MLLMs) presents a transformative opportunity to integrate a powerful cognitive engine into AD systems, moving beyond pattern matching toward genuine comprehension. However, a systematic framework to guide this integration is critically lacking. To bridge this gap, we provide a comprehensive review of this emerging field and argue that reasoning should be elevated from a modular component to the system's cognitive core. Specifically, we first propose a novel Cognitive Hierarchy to decompose the monolithic driving task according to its cognitive and interactive complexity. Building on this, we further derive and systematize seven core reasoning challenges, such as the responsiveness-reasoning trade-off and social-game reasoning. Furthermore, we conduct a dual-perspective review of the state-of-the-art, analyzing both system-centric approaches to architecting intelligent agents and evaluation-centric practices for their validation. Our analysis reveals a clear trend toward holistic and interpretable "glass-box" agents. In conclusion, we identify a fundamental and unresolved tension between the high-latency, deliberative nature of LLM-based reasoning and the millisecond-scale, safety-critical demands of vehicle control. For future work, a primary objective is to bridge the symbolic-to-physical gap by developing verifiable neuro-symbolic architectures, robust reasoning under uncertainty, and scalable models for implicit social negotiation.

</details>


### [24] [PACED: Distillation at the Frontier of Student Competence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11178)
*Yuanda Xu,Hejian Sang,Zhengze Zhou,Ran He,Zhipeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Paced框架通过引入基于pass-rate的权重函数w(p)=p^α(1-p)^β解决LLM蒸馏中的计算浪费问题，将学习重点集中在学生模型的"最近发展区"。该方法在蒸馏、自蒸馏和两阶段场景中均取得显著改进，且仅需学生rollout估计pass rate，无需架构修改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LLM蒸馏在两方面浪费计算：1)学生已掌握的问题产生近零梯度；2)超出学生能力的问题产生不相干梯度，侵蚀已有能力。这种浪费是结构性的——蒸馏梯度信噪比(SNR)在通过率两端必然消失。

Method: 提出Paced框架，使用原则性pass-rate权重w(p)=p^α(1-p)^β（Beta核），将蒸馏重点集中在学生能力的"最近发展区"。该权重函数源自蒸馏梯度的边界消失结构。方法仅需学生rollouts估计pass rate，无需架构修改，兼容任何KL方向。

Result: 理论方面：证明Beta核是从蒸馏SNR结构导出的主导权重族，在有界乘性错误指定下，最差效率损失仅为O(δ²)。蒸馏任务：使用前向KL从大教师蒸馏到小学生，显著超越基线，保持低遗忘。自蒸馏任务：反向KL下超越基线。两阶段协同：前向KL然后反向KL的调度产生最佳结果，在标准推理基准上取得实质性改进。

Conclusion: Paced框架通过聚焦学生模型的最近发展区，有效解决了蒸馏计算浪费的核心问题。理论证明Beta核是最优权重族且具有鲁棒性，实验验证了在多种蒸馏场景中的有效性，特别是两阶段前向KL→反向KL调度模式。该方法简单实用，仅需估计pass rate即可实现显著性能提升。

Abstract: Standard LLM distillation wastes compute on two fronts: problems the student has already mastered (near-zero gradients) and problems far beyond its reach (incoherent gradients that erode existing capabilities). We show that this waste is not merely intuitive but structurally inevitable: the gradient signal-to-noise ratio in distillation provably vanishes at both pass-rate extremes. This theoretical observation leads to Paced, a framework that concentrates distillation on the zone of proximal development -- the frontier of a student model's competence -- via a principled pass-rate weight $w(p) = p^α(1 - p)^β$ derived from the boundary-vanishing structure of distillation gradients. Key results: (1) Theory: We prove that the Beta kernel $w(p) = p^α(1-p)^β$ is a leading-order weight family arising from the SNR structure of distillation, and that it is minimax-robust -- under bounded multiplicative misspecification, worst-case efficiency loss is only $O(δ^2)$. (2)Distillation: On distillation from a larger teacher to a smaller student model with forward KL, Paced achieves significant gain over the base model, while keeping benchmark forgetting at a low level. (3)Self-distillation: On instruction-tuned models with reverse KL, gains are exceeding baselines as well. (4)Two-stage synergy: A forward-KL-then-reverse-KL schedule yields the strongest results in our setting, reaching substantial improvements on standard reasoning benchmarks -- supporting a mode-coverage-then-consolidation interpretation of the distillation process. All configurations require only student rollouts to estimate pass rates, need no architectural changes, and are compatible with any KL direction.

</details>


### [25] [The Unlearning Mirage: A Dynamic Framework for Evaluating LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11266)
*Raj Sanjay Shah,Jing Huang,Keerthiram Murugesan,Nathalie Baracaldo,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM遗忘学习方法的脆弱性问题，提出一个动态评估框架。该框架通过构建从简单到复杂的多跳查询链来压力测试遗忘鲁棒性，发现多跳查询往往使用替代路径绕开被破坏的主路径，解释了现有遗忘技术的 brittleness（脆弱性）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型语言模型(LLM)的遗忘学习(unlearning)方法存在脆弱性问题：轻微的查询修改（如多跳推理和实体别名）就能恢复本应被遗忘的信息。现有评估指标依赖静态、非结构化基准，存在虚假的有效性评估，无法检测这些漏洞。

Method: 提出一个动态框架，使用复杂结构化查询对遗忘鲁棒性进行压力测试。首先从目标模型（遗忘前）唤起知识，构建针对性探测，从简单查询到多跳链式查询，实现对查询难度的精确控制。

Result: （1）通过自动生成语义等价的问答探测，展示与现有基准相当的覆盖率；（2）与先前的评估保持一致；（3）发现了其他基准遗漏的新的遗忘失败案例，特别是在多跳设置中。

Conclusion: 单跳查询通常遵循主导计算路径，更容易被遗忘方法破坏；而多跳查询倾向于使用替代路径，这些路径往往保持完整，从而解释了遗忘技术在多跳设置中的脆弱性。该框架实现了无需人工构建遗忘测试集即可进行实际、可扩展的评估。

Abstract: Unlearning in Large Language Models (LLMs) aims to enhance safety, mitigate biases, and comply with legal mandates, such as the right to be forgotten. However, existing unlearning methods are brittle: minor query modifications, such as multi-hop reasoning and entity aliasing, can recover supposedly forgotten information. As a result, current evaluation metrics often create an illusion of effectiveness, failing to detect these vulnerabilities due to reliance on static, unstructured benchmarks. We propose a dynamic framework that stress tests unlearning robustness using complex structured queries. Our approach first elicits knowledge from the target model (pre-unlearning) and constructs targeted probes, ranging from simple queries to multi-hop chains, allowing precise control over query difficulty. Our experiments show that the framework (1) shows comparable coverage to existing benchmarks by automatically generating semantically equivalent Q&A probes, (2) aligns with prior evaluations, and (3) uncovers new unlearning failures missed by other benchmarks, particularly in multi-hop settings. Furthermore, activation analyses show that single-hop queries typically follow dominant computation pathways, which are more likely to be disrupted by unlearning methods. In contrast, multi-hop queries tend to use alternative pathways that often remain intact, explaining the brittleness of unlearning techniques in multi-hop settings. Our framework enables practical and scalable evaluation of unlearning methods without the need for manual construction of forget test sets, enabling easier adoption for real-world applications. We release the pip package and the code at https://sites.google.com/view/unlearningmirage/home.

</details>


### [26] [From Debate to Deliberation: Structured Collective Reasoning with Typed Epistemic Acts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11781)
*Sunil Prakash*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Deliberative Collective Intelligence (DCI) 框架，通过四类推理原型、14种认知行为类型、共享工作空间和DCI-CF收敛算法，为多智能体LLM系统引入结构化审议机制。实验表明DCI在非结构化任务上显著优于无结构辩论，尤其在隐藏画像任务上表现最佳(9.56)，但消耗约62倍单智能体token，且整体质量略逊于单智能体生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多智能体LLM系统的交互模式仅限于投票、无结构辩论或管道编排，缺乏对审议过程的建模。审议是一种阶段性过程，参与者交换不同类型的推理行为、保留分歧、并产生可问责的结果。该研究旨在弥补这一空白。

Method: 提出DCI框架，包含：(1)四类推理原型；(2)14种认知行为类型；(3)共享工作空间；(4)DCI-CF收敛流算法，确保终止并产生结构化决策包（含选选项、残留异议、少数派报告和重新开放条件）。使用Gemini 2.5 Flash在7个领域45个任务上评估。

Result: 在非结构化任务(n=40)上，DCI显著优于无结构辩论(+0.95, 95%CI[+0.41,+1.54])；隐藏画像任务表现最佳(9.56)，但常规决策表现差(5.39)；产生100%结构化决策包和98%少数派报告；消耗约62倍单智能体token；单智能体生成整体质量优于DCI。

Conclusion: DCI的核心贡献不在于"更多智能体更好"，而在于当过程问责性合理时，重大决策可从审议结构中获益。研究证实了任务依赖性——DCI适用于需要视角整合的复杂非结构化任务，不适用于常规决策。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems increasingly tackle complex reasoning, yet their interaction patterns remain limited to voting, unstructured debate, or pipeline orchestration. None model deliberation: a phased process where differentiated participants exchange typed reasoning moves, preserve disagreements, and converge on accountable outcomes. We introduce Deliberative Collective Intelligence (DCI), specifying four reasoning archetypes, 14 typed epistemic acts, a shared workspace, and DCI-CF, a convergent flow algorithm that guarantees termination with a structured decision packet containing the selected option, residual objections, minority report, and reopen conditions. We evaluate on 45 tasks across seven domains using Gemini 2.5 Flash. On non-routine tasks (n=40), DCI significantly improves over unstructured debate (+0.95, 95% CI [+0.41, +1.54]). DCI excels on hidden-profile tasks requiring perspective integration (9.56, highest of any system on any domain) while failing on routine decisions (5.39), confirming task-dependence. DCI produces 100% structured decision packets and 98% minority reports, artifacts absent from all baselines. However, DCI consumes ~62x single-agent tokens, and single-agent generation outperforms DCI on overall quality. DCI's contribution is not that more agents are better, but that consequential decisions benefit from deliberative structure when process accountability justifies the cost.

</details>


### [27] [Counterweights and Complementarities: The Convergence of AI and Blockchain Powering a Decentralized Future](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11299)
*Yibai Li,Zhiye Jin,Xiaobing,Li,K. D. Joshi,Xuefei,Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This editorial argues for the integration of AI and blockchain technologies to create "decentralized intelligence" (DI), where blockchain's decentralization counters AI's centralizing tendencies, and AI enhances blockchain efficiency. The two technologies are presented as complementary rather than competing.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from concerns about AI's centralizing tendencies, particularly with large language models (LLMs) creating data and resource monopolies controlled by large corporations. The authors seek to address this imbalance by proposing blockchain as a counterbalancing mechanism.

Method: This is an editorial paper presenting a conceptual analysis and argument. It uses comparative analysis to examine the opposing tendencies of AI (centralization) and blockchain (decentralization), and proposes a synergistic framework for combining their strengths.

Result: The paper establishes the complementary relationship between AI and blockchain: blockchain can mitigate AI's centralization risks through decentralized data management, computation, and governance; AI can enhance blockchain through automated smart contract management, content curation, and threat detection.

Conclusion: The paper concludes that AI and blockchain should be synergistically developed together to create "decentralized intelligence" (DI) - an interdisciplinary research area aimed at building intelligent systems without centralized control, balancing the efficiency of AI with the decentralized nature of blockchain.

Abstract: This editorial addresses the critical intersection of artificial intelligence (AI) and blockchain technologies, highlighting their contrasting tendencies toward centralization and decentralization, respectively. While AI, particularly with the rise of large language models (LLMs), exhibits a strong centralizing force due to data and resource monopolization by large corporations, blockchain offers a counterbalancing mechanism through its inherent decentralization, transparency, and security. The editorial argues that these technologies are not mutually exclusive but possess complementary strengths. Blockchain can mitigate AI's centralizing risks by enabling decentralized data management, computation, and governance, promoting greater inclusivity, transparency, and user privacy. Conversely, AI can enhance blockchain's efficiency and security through automated smart contract management, content curation, and threat detection. The core argument calls for the development of ``decentralized intelligence'' (DI) -- an interdisciplinary research area focused on creating intelligent systems that function without centralized control.

</details>


### [28] [FinRule-Bench: A Benchmark for Joint Reasoning over Financial Tables and Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11339)
*Arun Vignesh Malarkkan,Manan Roy Choudhury,Guangwei Zhang,Vivek Gupta,Qingyun Wang,Yanjie Fu,Denghui Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FinRule-Bench是一个评估大语言模型在真实财务表格上进行基于规则的财务推理的基准测试，涵盖资产负债表、现金流量表、损益表和股东权益表四种报表类型，设置三个递进难度的审计任务：规则验证、规则识别和联合规则诊断。研究发现模型在孤立规则验证上表现良好，但在规则判别和多违规诊断上性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在财务分析中的应用日益增多，但其根据明确会计原则审计结构化财务报表的能力尚未得到充分探索。现有基准测试主要评估问答、数值推理或合成损坏数据上的异常检测，无法确定模型能否在正确的财务报表上可靠地验证或定位规则合规性。

Method: 构建FinRule-Bench基准测试，将真实财务报表与人工策划的会计原则配对，涵盖四种典型报表类型。定义三个需要递进增强推理能力的审计任务：(i)规则验证-测试单条原则的合规性；(ii)规则识别-从给定规则集中选择违规原则；(iii)联合规则诊断-在记录级别检测和定位多个同时违规。采用零样本和少样本提示评估LLMs，并引入因果反事实推理协议以确保决策、解释和反事实判断之间的一致性。

Result: 跨任务和报表类型的评估表明：模型在孤立规则验证任务上表现良好，但在规则判别和多违规诊断任务上性能急剧下降。

Conclusion: FinRule-Bench为研究高风险财务分析中LLM的规则governed推理、诊断覆盖率和失败模式提供了一个原则性和可复现的测试平台。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to financial analysis, yet their ability to audit structured financial statements under explicit accounting principles remains poorly explored. Existing benchmarks primarily evaluate question answering, numerical reasoning, or anomaly detection on synthetically corrupted data, making it unclear whether models can reliably verify or localize rule compliance on correct financial statements. We introduce FinRule-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating diagnostic completeness in rule-based financial reasoning over real-world financial tables. FinRule-Bench pairs ground-truth financial statements with explicit, human-curated accounting principles and spans four canonical statement types: Balance Sheets, Cash Flow Statements, Income Statements, and Statements of Equity. The benchmark defines three auditing tasks that require progressively stronger reasoning capabilities: (i) rule verification, which tests compliance with a single principle; (ii) rule identification, which requires selecting the violated principle from a provided rule set; and (iii) joint rule diagnosis, which requires detecting and localizing multiple simultaneous violations at the record level. We evaluate LLMs under zero-shot and few-shot prompting, and introduce a causal-counterfactual reasoning protocol that enforces consistency between decisions, explanations, and counterfactual judgments. Across tasks and statement types, we find that while models perform well on isolated rule verification, performance degrades sharply for rule discrimination and multi-violation diagnosis. FinRule-Bench provides a principled and reproducible testbed for studying rule-governed reasoning, diagnostic coverage, and failure modes of LLMs in high-stakes financial analysis.

</details>


### [29] [Improving LLM Performance Through Black-Box Online Tuning: A Case for Adding System Specs to Factsheets for Trusted AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11340)
*Yonas Atinafu,Henry Lin,Robin Cohen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的黑盒在线控制器,仅使用短段的端到端测量(无需内部仪表化)并通过爬山算法最大化goodput(即满足服务级别目标的请求吞吐量),并以LLM服务为具体示例,同时讨论了将系统性能和可持续性指标集成到AI系统Factsheets中的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM服务等复杂系统中,传统方法依赖内部仪器化或详细系统模型,实现成本高且缺乏实用性。需要一种无需修改系统内部结构即可优化性能的黑盒方法。

Method: 提出一种黑盒在线控制器,仅通过端到端测量获取短时段数据,采用爬山算法优化goodput(满足SLO的请求吞吐量),无需系统内部仪表化。

Result: 提供了经验证据证明该设计的有效性,并以LLM服务为例展示了实际应用价值。

Conclusion: 黑盒控制器是一种可行且有效的性能优化方案;同时,AI系统的Factsheets应纳入系统性能和可持续性指标,以促进组织采用负责任的AI系统。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a novel black-box online controller that uses only end-to-end measurements over short segments, without internal instrumentation, and hill climbing to maximize goodput, defined as the throughput of requests that satisfy the service-level objective. We provide empirical evidence that this design is well-founded. Using this advance in LLM serving as a concrete example, we then discuss the importance of integrating system performance and sustainability metrics into Factsheets for organizations adopting AI systems.

</details>
